Value

Paper alerts, not advice. A DK/FD price is alerted when it has not moved since the news and the repriced consensus (Pinnacle when fresh, else the median of fresh books) beats it by 3 pp (game markets) or 6 pp (props); more with a two-book or model-only price. Graded by CLV against the consensus close; a signal is judged at 200 graded alerts (an early read at 60).

Model picks (research only)

Since M18 no model-vs-market pick is flagged. A pick is listed and graded, for research, when it clears the threshold chosen on validation seasons:

  • spread: model and market differ by ≥ 3 pts. Research only since M18: never flagged; the card notes whether Pinnacle's no-vig line or Kalshi sides with the pick
  • moneyline: edges shown for information only, never flagged: moneyline flags need a positive final-test ROI (it was -9.2%)

Season record

What is counted: One pick per game and market: the model's last prediction before the start, counted whenever its edge cleared the threshold below (the flag rule), whether or not a flag was shown. Beating the close: share of picks that got a better number than the closing line, among picks whose number differed from it. W-L-P: wins, losses, pushes. Under 100 picks a record is too few to judge.

Value

Paper alerts, not advice. A DK/FD price is alerted when it has not moved since the news and the repriced consensus (Pinnacle when fresh, else the median of fresh books) beats it by 3 pp (game markets) or 6 pp (props); more with a two-book or model-only price. Graded by CLV against the consensus close; a signal is judged at 200 graded alerts (an early read at 60).

Model picks (research only)

Since M18 no model-vs-market pick is flagged. A pick is listed and graded, for research, when it clears the threshold chosen on validation seasons:

  • spread: model and market differ by ≥ 6 pts. Research only since M18: never flagged; the card notes whether Pinnacle's no-vig line or Kalshi sides with the pick
  • moneyline: edges shown for information only, never flagged: moneyline flags need a positive final-test ROI (it was -7.0%)

Season record

What is counted: One pick per game and market: the model's last prediction before the start, counted whenever its edge cleared the threshold below (the flag rule), whether or not a flag was shown. Beating the close: share of picks that got a better number than the closing line, among picks whose number differed from it. W-L-P: wins, losses, pushes. Under 100 picks a record is too few to judge.
